/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */

185 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.

191 |     JsDocToken token = next();

195 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
196 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
197 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
198 |       token = blockInfo.token;
199 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
200 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
201 |       }
202 |     } else {
203 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
204 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
205 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
206 |         // what it was.
207 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");

212 |     retry: for (;;) {
213 |       switch (token) {

215 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
216 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;

218 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
219 | 
220 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
221 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
222 |             if (annotation == null) {
223 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
224 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
225 |             } else {

227 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);

229 |               switch (annotation) {

231 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
232 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
233 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;

236 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",

242 |                   } else {

244 |                   }

246 | 
247 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
248 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
249 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
250 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |                   }
252 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
253 |                   continue retry;

255 |                 case CONSTANT:
256 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
257 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
258 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
259 |                   }
260 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
261 |                   continue retry;

263 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
264 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
265 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
266 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
267 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

271 |                     }
272 |                   }

276 |                 case DEPRECATED:
277 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {

279 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
280 |                   }
281 |

283 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
284 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
285 | 
286 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
287 | 
288 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {

290 |                   }

297 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
298 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
299 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
300 |                     } else {

303 |                     }

316 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);

809 |                 case DEFINE:
810 |                 case RETURN:

812 |                 case TYPE:
813 |                 case TYPEDEF:
814 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
815 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
816 | 
817 |                   Node typeNode = null;
818 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
819 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
820 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
821 |                     // it as the unknown type.

831 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
832 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);

836 | 
837 |                   if (type == null) {
838 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
839 |                     // recovering parsing
840 |                   } else {
841 |                     switch (annotation) {
842 |                       case DEFINE:
843 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
844 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
845 |                               lineno, charno);
846 |                         }
847 |                         break;
848 | 
849 |                       case RETURN:
850 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
851 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
852 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
853 |                           break;
854 |                         }
855 | 
856 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
857 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
858 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
859 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
860 | 
861 |                           String returnDescription =
862 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
863 | 
864 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
865 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
866 |                                 returnDescription);
867 |                           }
868 | 
869 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
870 |                         } else {
871 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
872 |                         }
873 |                         continue retry;
874 | 
875 |                       case THIS:
876 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
877 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
878 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
879 |                         }
880 |                         break;
881 | 
882 |                       case TYPE:
883 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
884 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
885 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
886 |                         }
887 |                         break;
888 | 
889 |                       case TYPEDEF:
890 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
891 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
892 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
893 |                         }
894 |                         break;
895 |                     }
896 |                   }
897 | 
898 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
899 |                   continue retry;
900 |               }
901 |             }
902 |           }
903 |           break;
904 | 
905 |         case EOC:
906 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
907 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
908 |           }
909 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
910 |           return true;
911 | 
912 |         case EOF:
913 |           // discard any accumulated information
914 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
915 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
916 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
917 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
918 |           return false;
919 | 
920 |         case EOL:
921 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
922 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
923 |           }
924 |           token = next();
925 |           continue retry;
926 | 
927 |         default:
928 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
929 |             token = next();
930 |             continue retry;
931 |           } else {
932 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
933 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
934 |             continue retry;
935 |           }
936 |       }
937 | 
938 |       // next token
939 |       token = next();
940 |     }
941 |   }

   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.

===== M0012 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1452-1559
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1452 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1453 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1454 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1455 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1456 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1457 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1458 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1459 | 
1460 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1461 |       // 1) @type annnotation / @enum annotation
1462 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1463 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1464 |       // 4) ASSIGN to something marked @const
1465 |       // 5) ASSIGN to anything else
1466 |       //
1467 |       // 1, 3, and 4 are declarations, 5 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1468 |       // the function has jsdoc or has not been declared before.
1469 |       //
1470 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1471 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1472 | 
1473 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3 + #4
1474 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredType(t.getSourceName(), info, n, rhsValue);
1475 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1476 |         // Determining type for #5
1477 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1478 |       }
1479 | 
1480 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1481 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1482 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1483 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1484 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1485 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1486 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1487 |         if (qVar != null) {
1488 |           // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1489 |           // and they assign F.prototype to an object literal,
1490 |           // then they are responsible for making sure that the object literal's
1491 |           // implicit prototype is set up appropriately. We just obey
1492 |           // the @extends tag.
1493 |           ObjectType qVarType = ObjectType.cast(qVar.getType());
1494 |           if (qVarType != null &&
1495 |               rhsValue != null &&
1496 |               rhsValue.isObjectLit()) {
1497 |             typeRegistry.resetImplicitPrototype(
1498 |                 rhsValue.getJSType(), qVarType.getImplicitPrototype());
1499 |           } else if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1500 |             // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1501 |             // and they assign F.prototype to some arbitrary expression,
1502 |             // there's not much we can do. We just ignore the expression,
1503 |             // and hope they've annotated their code in a way to tell us
1504 |             // what props are going to be on that prototype.
1505 |             return;
1506 |           }
1507 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1508 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1509 |           }
1510 |         }
1511 |       }
1512 | 
1513 |       if (valueType == null) {
1514 |         if (parent.isExprResult()) {
1515 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1516 |               n,
1517 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1518 |               ownerName));
1519 |         }
1520 | 
1521 |         return;
1522 |       }
1523 | 
1524 |       boolean inferred = isQualifiedNameInferred(
1525 |           qName, n, info, rhsValue, valueType);
1526 |       if (!inferred) {
1527 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1528 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1529 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1530 |           // declared yet.
1531 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1532 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1533 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1534 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1535 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1536 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1537 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, n);
1538 |           }
1539 |         }
1540 | 
1541 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1542 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1543 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1544 |       } else if (rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isTrue()) {
1545 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1546 |         FunctionType ownerType =
1547 |             JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(getObjectSlot(ownerName));
1548 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1549 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ownerType.getTypeOfThis();
1550 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1551 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1552 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1553 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1554 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1555 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE), true);
1556 |           }
1557 |         }
1558 |       }
1559 |     }

===== M0018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 511-556
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
512 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
513 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
514 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
515 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
516 |     if (objectType == null) {
517 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
518 |     } else {
519 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
520 |         return;
521 |       }
522 | 
523 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
524 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
525 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
526 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
527 |         //
528 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
529 |         //    inferred type.
530 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
531 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
532 |         //    define it.
533 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
534 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
535 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
536 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
537 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
538 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
539 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(
540 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
541 |           } else {
542 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
543 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
544 |           }
545 |         } else {
546 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().isThis() &&
547 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
548 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
549 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
550 |           } else {
551 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
552 |           }
553 |         }
554 |       }
555 |     }
556 |   }

===== M0041 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 74-129
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
74 |   @Override
75 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
76 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
77 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
78 |     if (type != null) {
79 |       Node subclass = null;
80 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
81 | 
82 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
83 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
84 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
85 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
86 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
87 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
88 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
89 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
90 |           callName.isGetProp();
91 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
92 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
93 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
94 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
95 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
96 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
97 |       } else {
98 |         return null;
99 |       }
100 | 
101 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
102 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
103 |         // inheritance.
104 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
105 |           return null;
106 |         }
107 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
108 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
109 |             return null;
110 |           }
111 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
112 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
113 |         }
114 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
115 |       }
116 | 
117 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
118 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
119 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
120 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
121 |       if (subclass != null &&
122 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
123 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
124 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
125 |       }
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     return null;
129 |   }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType,String,JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1019-1120
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor type and a property name, check that the property has
   * the JSDoc annotation @override iff the property is declared on a
   * superclass. Several checks regarding inheritance correctness are also
   * performed.
   */
Method code:
1019 |   private void checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
1020 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, FunctionType ctorType, String propertyName,
1021 |       JSDocInfo info, JSType propertyType) {
1022 |     // If the supertype doesn't resolve correctly, we've warned about this
1023 |     // already.
1024 |     if (hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(ctorType)) {
1025 |       return;
1026 |     }
1027 | 
1028 |     FunctionType superClass = ctorType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1029 |     boolean superClassHasProperty = superClass != null &&
1030 |         superClass.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1031 |     // For interface
1032 |     boolean superInterfacesHasProperty = false;
1033 |     if (ctorType.isInterface()) {
1034 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1035 |         superInterfacesHasProperty =
1036 |           superInterfacesHasProperty || interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName);
1037 |       }
1038 |     }
1039 |     boolean declaredOverride = info != null && info.isOverride();
1040 | 
1041 |     boolean foundInterfaceProperty = false;
1042 |     if (ctorType.isConstructor()) {
1043 |       for (JSType implementedInterface :
1044 |           ctorType.getAllImplementedInterfaces()) {
1045 |         if (implementedInterface.isUnknownType() ||
1046 |             implementedInterface.isEmptyType()) {
1047 |           continue;
1048 |         }
1049 |         FunctionType interfaceType =
1050 |             implementedInterface.toObjectType().getConstructor();
1051 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(interfaceType);
1052 |         boolean interfaceHasProperty =
1053 |             interfaceType.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1054 |         foundInterfaceProperty = foundInterfaceProperty || interfaceHasProperty;
1055 |         if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && !declaredOverride &&
1056 |             interfaceHasProperty) {
1057 |           // @override not present, but the property does override an interface
1058 |           // property
1059 |           compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1060 |               HIDDEN_INTERFACE_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1061 |               interfaceType.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName).toString()));
1062 |         }
1063 |       }
1064 |     }
1065 | 
1066 |     if (!declaredOverride && !superClassHasProperty
1067 |         && !superInterfacesHasProperty) {
1068 |       // nothing to do here, it's just a plain new property
1069 |       return;
1070 |     }
1071 | 
1072 |     JSType topInstanceType = superClassHasProperty ?
1073 |         superClass.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName) : null;
1074 |     if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && ctorType.isConstructor() &&
1075 |         !declaredOverride && superClassHasProperty) {
1076 |       // @override not present, but the property does override a superclass
1077 |       // property
1078 |       compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1079 |           HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1080 |           topInstanceType.toString()));
1081 |     }
1082 |     if (!declaredOverride) {
1083 |       // there's no @override to check
1084 |       return;
1085 |     }
1086 |     // @override is present and we have to check that it is ok
1087 |     if (superClassHasProperty) {
1088 |       // there is a superclass implementation
1089 |       JSType superClassPropType =
1090 |           superClass.getPrototype().getPropertyType(propertyName);
1091 |       if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superClassPropType)) {
1092 |         compiler.report(
1093 |             t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1094 |                 propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1095 |                 superClassPropType.toString(), propertyType.toString()));
1096 |       }
1097 |     } else if (superInterfacesHasProperty) {
1098 |       // there is an super interface property
1099 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1100 |         if (interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName)) {
1101 |           JSType superPropertyType =
1102 |             interfaceType.getPropertyType(propertyName);
1103 |           if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superPropertyType)) {
1104 |             topInstanceType = interfaceType.getConstructor().
1105 |                 getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName);
1106 |             compiler.report(
1107 |                 t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1108 |                     propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1109 |                     superPropertyType.toString(),
1110 |                     propertyType.toString()));
1111 |           }
1112 |         }
1113 |       }
1114 |     } else if (!foundInterfaceProperty) {
1115 |       // there is no superclass nor interface implementation
1116 |       compiler.report(

===== M0078 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0082 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1014-1042
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

===== M0088 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 164-180
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
165 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
166 | 
167 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
168 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
169 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
170 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
171 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
172 |       }
173 | 
174 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
175 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
176 |       if (first.isName() &&

===== M0096 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1835-1859
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1835 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1836 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1837 |     Preconditions.checkState(!n.isFunction() ||
1838 |             type.isFunctionType() ||
1839 |             type.isUnknownType());
1840 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1841 |     if (info != null) {
1842 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1843 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1844 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1845 |         type = infoType;
1846 |       }
1847 | 
1848 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1849 |         String propName = n.isGetProp() ?
1850 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1851 |         compiler.report(
1852 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1853 |       }
1854 |     }
1855 | 
1856 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1857 |       n.setJSType(type);
1858 |     }
1859 |   }

===== M0103 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 828-855
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
828 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
829 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
830 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
831 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
832 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
833 |           return;
834 |         }
835 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
836 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
837 | 
838 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
839 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
840 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
841 |           } else {
842 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           }
844 |         } else { // Has a catch.
845 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
846 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
847 |             return;
848 |           } else {
849 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
850 |           }
851 |         }
852 |         lastJump = handler;
853 |       }
854 |     }
855 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
469 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
470 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
471 |          current != null;
472 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
473 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
474 |         return true;
475 |       }
476 |     }
477 |     return false;
478 |   }

===== M0108 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarro sub-lattice. See the commants on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }

===== M0109 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 546-576
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
546 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
547 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
548 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
549 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
550 |     if (isOptional) {
551 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
552 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
553 |       // error can be reported.
554 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
555 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
556 |         emittedWarning = true;
557 |       }
558 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
559 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
560 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
561 |         emittedWarning = true;
562 |       }
563 |     } else {
564 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
565 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
566 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
567 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
568 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
569 |         } else {
570 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
571 |         }
572 |         emittedWarning = true;
573 |       }
574 |     }
575 |     return emittedWarning;
576 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 365-408

/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
365 |   boolean setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
366 |     if (prototype == null) {
367 |       return false;
368 |     }
369 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
370 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
371 |       return false;
372 |     }

375 |         ? null : (PrototypeObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
376 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;

379 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
380 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
381 |

383 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
384 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
385 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
386 |     }

389 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
390 |       if (superClass != null) {

392 |       }
393 |

396 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
397 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
398 |           }
399 |         }
400 |       }
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
404 |       clearCachedValues();
405 |     }
406 | 
407 |     return true;
408 |   }

===== M0119 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.checkForClassDefiningCalls(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1332-1399
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for class-defining calls.
     * Because JS has no 'native' syntax for defining classes,
     * this is often very coding-convention dependent and business-logic heavy.
     */
Method code:
1332 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(
1333 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1334 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
1335 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
1336 |       if (relationship != null) {
1337 |         FunctionType superCtor = getFunctionType(
1338 |             scope.getVar(relationship.superclassName));
1339 |         FunctionType subCtor = getFunctionType(
1340 |             scope.getVar(relationship.subclassName));
1341 |         if (superCtor != null && superCtor.isConstructor() &&
1342 |             subCtor != null && subCtor.isConstructor()) {
1343 |           ObjectType superClass = superCtor.getInstanceType();
1344 |           ObjectType subClass = subCtor.getInstanceType();
1345 | 
1346 |           // superCtor and subCtor might be structural constructors
1347 |           // (like {function(new:Object)}) so we need to resolve them back
1348 |           // to the original ctor objects.
1349 |           superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
1350 |           subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
1351 | 
1352 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS &&
1353 |               !superClass.isEmptyType() && !subClass.isEmptyType()) {
1354 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
1355 |           }
1356 | 
1357 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
1358 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
1359 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
1360 |           }
1361 |         }
1362 |       }
1363 | 
1364 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
1365 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
1366 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
1367 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
1368 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
1369 |         if (objectType != null) {
1370 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
1371 | 
1372 |           if (functionType != null) {
1373 |             FunctionType getterType =
1374 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
1375 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
1376 |                 objectType);
1377 |           }
1378 |         }
1379 |       }
1380 | 
1381 |       DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
1382 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
1383 |       if (delegateRelationship != null) {
1384 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);
1385 |       }
1386 | 
1387 |       ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
1388 |           codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(t, n);
1389 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
1390 |         ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1391 |             typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
1392 |         if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
1393 |           setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
1394 |         } else {

Original range: 228-291

/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
228 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
229 |     this.options = options;
230 |     if (errorManager == null) {
231 |       if (outStream == null) {
232 |         setErrorManager(
233 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
234 |       } else {
235 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
236 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
237 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
238 |         setErrorManager(printer);
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
243 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
244 |       options.checkTypes = true;
245 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
246 |       options.checkTypes = false;
247 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
248 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
249 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
250 |       // parser type warnings.
251 |       options.setWarningLevel(
252 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
253 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
254 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
258 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
259 |       options.setWarningLevel(
260 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
261 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
262 |     }
263 | 
264 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
265 |       options.setWarningLevel(
266 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
267 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
268 |     }
269 | 
270 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
271 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
272 |     guards.add(
273 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
274 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
275 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
276 | 
277 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
278 | 
279 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
280 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
281 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
282 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
283 |     // error off.
284 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
285 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
286 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
287 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
288 |     }
289 | 
290 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
291 |   }

===== M0124 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1659-1698
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1659 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1660 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1661 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1662 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1663 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1664 |       // {?} - right curly
1665 |       // {?=} - equals
1666 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1667 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1668 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1669 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1670 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1671 |       token = next();
1672 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1673 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1674 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1675 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1676 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1677 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1678 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1679 |       }
1680 | 
1681 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1682 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1683 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1684 |     } else {
1685 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1686 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1687 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1688 |           next();
1689 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1690 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1691 |           next();
1692 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1693 |         }
1694 |       }
1695 | 
1696 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1697 |     }
1698 |   }

===== M0128 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 398-411
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
398 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
399 |       Node ret;
400 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
401 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
402 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
403 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
404 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
405 |       } else {
406 |         ret = transform(n);
407 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
408 |       }
409 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
410 |       return ret;
411 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 240-266
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect the type to be anything but the null or void type. If the
   * expectation is not met, issue a warning at the provided node's
   * source code position. Note that a union type that includes the
   * void type and at least one other type meets the expectation.
   * @return Whether the expectation was met.
   */
Method code:
240 |   boolean expectNotNullOrUndefined(
241 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, String msg, JSType expectedType) {
242 |     if (!type.isNoType() && !type.isUnknownType() &&
243 |         type.isSubtype(nullOrUndefined) &&
244 |         !containsForwardDeclaredUnresolvedName(type)) {
245 | 
246 |       // There's one edge case right now that we don't handle well, and
247 |       // that we don't want to warn about.
248 |       // if (this.x == null) {
249 |       //   this.initializeX();
250 |       //   this.x.foo();
251 |       // }
252 |       // In this case, we incorrectly type x because of how we
253 |       // infer properties locally. See issue 109.
254 |       // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=109
255 |       //
256 |       // We do not do this inference globally.
257 |       if (n.isGetProp() &&
258 |           !t.inGlobalScope() && type.isNullType()) {
259 |         return true;
260 |       }
261 | 
262 |       mismatch(t, n, msg, type, expectedType);
263 |       return false;
264 |     }
265 |     return true;
266 |   }

===== M0147 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 199-227
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
199 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
200 |     initialize();
201 |     int step = 0;
202 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
203 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
204 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
205 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
206 |       }
207 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
208 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
209 |       joinInputs(curNode);
210 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
211 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
212 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
213 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
214 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
215 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
216 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
217 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
218 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
219 |           }
220 |         }
221 |       }
222 |       step++;
223 |     }
224 |     if (isForward()) {
225 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
226 |     }
227 |   }

===== M0152 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.dereferencePointer(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1143-1152
Documentation:
/**
   * If we access a property of a symbol, then that symbol is not
   * null or undefined.
   */
Method code:
1143 |   private FlowScope dereferencePointer(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1144 |     if (n.isQualifiedName()) {
1145 |       JSType type = getJSType(n);
1146 |       JSType narrowed = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1147 |       if (type != narrowed) {
1148 |         scope = narrowScope(scope, n, narrowed);
1149 |       }

===== M0159 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2021-2033
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is used as an L-value. Notice that sometimes
   * names are used as both L-values and R-values.
   *
   * We treat "var x;" as a pseudo-L-value, which kind of makes sense if you
   * treat it as "assignment to 'undefined' at the top of the scope". But if
   * we're honest with ourselves, it doesn't make sense, and we only do this
   * because it makes sense to treat this as synactically similar to
   * "var x = 0;".
   *
   * @param n The node
   * @return True if n is an L-value.
   */
Method code:
2021 |   static boolean isLValue(Node n) {
2022 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isName() || n.isGetProp() ||
2023 |         n.isGetElem());
2024 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
2025 |     return (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2026 |         || (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2027 |         || parent.isVar()
2028 |         || (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2029 |         || parent.isDec()
2030 |         || parent.isInc()
2031 |         || parent.isParamList()
2032 |         || parent.isCatch();
2033 |   }

     * Declares all of a function's arguments.
     */
Method code:
1886 |     private void declareArguments(Node functionNode) {
1887 |       Node astParameters = functionNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
1888 |       Node body = astParameters.getNext();

1890 |           JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(functionNode.getJSType());

1893 |         if (jsDocParameters != null) {

1895 |           for (Node astParameter : astParameters.children()) {

1897 |               defineSlot(astParameter, functionNode,
1898 |                   jsDocParameter.getJSType(), false);
1899 |               jsDocParameter = jsDocParameter.getNext();
1900 |             } else {

138 |     @Override
139 |     public boolean apply(JSType type) {
140 |       ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(type);
141 |       if (objectType == null) {
142 |         reportWarning(EXTENDS_NON_OBJECT, fnName, type.toString());
143 |         return false;

===== M0184 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.attachLiteralTypes(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 557-603
Documentation:

Method code:
557 |     private void attachLiteralTypes(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
558 |       switch (n.getType()) {
559 |         case Token.NULL:
560 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NULL_TYPE));
561 |           break;
562 | 
563 |         case Token.VOID:
564 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
565 |           break;
566 | 
567 |         case Token.STRING:
568 |           // Defer keys to the Token.OBJECTLIT case
569 |           if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
570 |             n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
571 |           }
572 |           break;
573 | 
574 |         case Token.NUMBER:
575 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
576 |           break;
577 | 
578 |         case Token.TRUE:
579 |         case Token.FALSE:
580 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
581 |           break;
582 | 
583 |         case Token.REGEXP:
584 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(REGEXP_TYPE));
585 |           break;
586 | 
587 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
588 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
589 |           if (info != null &&
590 |               info.getLendsName() != null) {
591 |             if (lentObjectLiterals == null) {
592 |               lentObjectLiterals = Lists.newArrayList();
593 |             }
594 |             lentObjectLiterals.add(n);
595 |           } else {
596 |             defineObjectLiteral(n);
597 |           }
598 |           break;
599 | 
600 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): If we ever support Array tuples,
601 |           // we will need to put ARRAYLIT here as well.
602 |       }
603 |     }

===== M0194 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),

425 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
426 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
427 |     switch (n.getType()) {
428 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
429 |         // normal type checking
430 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
431 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
432 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
433 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
434 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
435 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
436 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
437 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
438 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
439 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
440 |         }
441 | 
442 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
443 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
444 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
445 |         break;
446 |     }
447 |     return true;
448 |   }

===== M0208 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 256-306
Documentation:

Method code:
256 |   @Override
257 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
258 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
259 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
260 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
261 |         return true;
262 |       }
263 | 
264 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
265 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
266 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
267 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
268 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
269 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
270 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
271 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
272 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
273 |         return false;
274 |       }
275 | 
276 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
277 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
278 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
279 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
280 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
281 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
282 |             return false;
283 |           }
284 |         }
285 | 
286 |         return true;
287 |       }
288 | 
289 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
290 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
291 | 
292 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
293 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
294 |           return false;
295 |         }
296 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
297 |       }
298 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
299 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
300 |           return false;
301 |         }
302 |       }
303 |       return true;
304 |     }
305 |     return false;
306 |   }

===== M0214 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkPropertyAccess(JSType,String,NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1286-1313
Documentation:
/**
   * Emit a warning if we can prove that a property cannot possibly be
   * defined on an object. Note the difference between JS and a strictly
   * statically typed language: we're checking if the property
   * *cannot be defined*, whereas a java compiler would check if the
   * property *can be undefined*.
   */
Method code:
1286 |   private void checkPropertyAccess(JSType childType, String propName,
1287 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1288 |     // If the property type is unknown, check the object type to see if it
1289 |     // can ever be defined. We explicitly exclude CHECKED_UNKNOWN (for
1290 |     // properties where we've checked that it exists, or for properties on
1291 |     // objects that aren't in this binary).
1292 |     JSType propType = getJSType(n);
1293 |     if (propType.equals(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1294 |       childType = childType.autobox();
1295 |       ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(childType);
1296 |       if (objectType != null) {
1297 |         // We special-case object types so that checks on enums can be
1298 |         // much stricter, and so that we can use hasProperty (which is much
1299 |         // faster in most cases).
1300 |         if (!objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
1301 |             objectType.equals(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1302 |           if (objectType instanceof EnumType) {
1303 |             report(t, n, INEXISTENT_ENUM_ELEMENT, propName);
1304 |           } else {
1305 |             checkPropertyAccessHelper(objectType, propName, t, n);
1306 |           }
1307 |         }
1308 | 
1309 |       } else {
1310 |         checkPropertyAccessHelper(childType, propName, t, n);
1311 |       }
1312 |     }
1313 |   }

===== M0219 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 177-221
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
177 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
178 |     // TODO(user): regex literals as well.
179 |     switch (n.getType()) {
180 |       case Token.STRING:
181 |         return n.getString();
182 | 
183 |       case Token.NAME:
184 |         String name = n.getString();
185 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
186 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
187 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
188 |           return name;
189 |         }
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NUMBER:
193 |         return getStringValue(n.getDouble());
194 | 
195 |       case Token.FALSE:
196 |         return "false";
197 | 
198 |       case Token.TRUE:
199 |         return "true";
200 | 
201 |       case Token.NULL:
202 |         return "null";
203 | 
204 |       case Token.VOID:
205 |         return "undefined";
206 | 
207 |       case Token.NOT:
208 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
209 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
210 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? "false" : "true"; // reversed.
211 |         }
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
215 |         return arrayToString(n);
216 | 
217 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
218 |         return "[object Object]";
219 |     }
220 |     return null;
221 |   }

1415 |   private void visitNew(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {

===== M0237 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 135-247
Documentation:

Method code:
135 |   @Override
136 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
137 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
138 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
139 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
140 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
141 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
142 |     // worthwhile.
143 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
144 |     Node condition = null;
145 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
146 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
147 | 
148 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
149 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
150 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
151 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
152 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
153 | 
154 |       switch (branch) {
155 |         case ON_TRUE:
156 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
157 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
158 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
159 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
160 | 
161 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
162 | 
163 |             if (item.isVar()) {
164 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
165 |             }
166 |             if (item.isName()) {
167 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
168 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
169 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
170 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
171 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
172 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
173 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
174 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
175 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
176 |                 }
177 |               }
178 |               redeclareSimpleVar(informed, item, iterKeyType);
179 |             }
180 |             newScope = informed;
181 |             break;
182 |           }
183 | 
184 |           // FALL THROUGH
185 | 
186 |         case ON_FALSE:
187 |           if (condition == null) {
188 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
189 |             if (condition == null && source.isCase()) {
190 |               condition = source;
191 | 
192 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
193 |               // of the loop.
194 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
195 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
196 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
197 |               }
198 |             }
199 |           }
200 | 
201 |           if (condition != null) {
202 |             if (condition.isAnd() ||
203 |                 condition.isOr()) {
204 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
205 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
206 |               // scope on false.
207 |               //
208 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
209 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
210 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
211 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
212 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
213 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
214 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
215 | 
216 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
217 |               // of the loop.
218 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
219 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.isAnd() ?
220 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
221 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
222 |               }
223 |               newScope =
224 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
225 |                       condition,
226 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
227 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
228 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
229 |             } else {
230 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
231 |               // of the loop.
232 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
233 |                 conditionFlowScope =
234 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
235 |               }
236 |               newScope =
237 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
238 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
239 |             }
240 |           }
241 |           break;
242 |       }
243 | 
244 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
245 |     }
246 |     return result;
247 |   }

===== M0245 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)

Original range: 441-453
Documentation:

Method code:
441 |     @Override
442 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
443 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
444 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
445 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
446 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
447 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
448 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
449 |           return true;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |       return false;
453 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 421-494
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
421 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
422 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
423 |     if (argsParent == null) {
424 |       if (info == null) {
425 |         return this;
426 |       } else {
427 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
428 |       }
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     // arguments
432 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
433 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
434 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
435 |     }
436 | 
437 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
438 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
439 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
440 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
441 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
442 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
443 |     boolean isVarArgs = false;
444 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
445 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
446 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
447 | 
448 |       // type from JSDocInfo
449 |       JSType parameterType = null;
450 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
451 |       isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
452 | 
453 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
454 |         parameterType =
455 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
456 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
457 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
458 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
459 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
460 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
461 |       } else {
462 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
463 |       }
464 | 
465 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
466 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
467 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
468 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
469 |         }
470 |         foundTemplateType = true;
471 |       }
472 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
473 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
474 |           isOptionalParam,
475 |           isVarArgs);
476 | 
477 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
478 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
479 |       }
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     // Copy over any old parameters that aren't in the param list.
483 | 
484 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
485 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
486 |     }
487 | 
488 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
489 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
493 |     return this;
494 |   }
