===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

===== M0254 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 833-932
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.isAssign()</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
833 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
834 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
835 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
836 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
837 | 
838 |     if (lvalue.isGetProp()) {
839 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
840 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
841 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
842 | 
843 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
844 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
845 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
846 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
847 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
848 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
849 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
850 |         }
851 |       }
852 | 
853 |       // /** @type ... */object.name = ...;
854 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
855 |         visitAnnotatedAssignGetprop(t, assign,
856 |             info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry), object,
857 |             property, rvalue);
858 |         return;
859 |       }
860 | 
861 |       checkEnumAlias(t, info, rvalue);
862 | 
863 |       // object.prototype = ...;
864 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
865 |         if (objectJsType != null && objectJsType.isFunctionType()) {
866 |           FunctionType functionType = objectJsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
867 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
868 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
869 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
870 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
871 |           }
872 |         } else {
873 |           // TODO(user): might want to flag that
874 |         }
875 |         return;
876 |       }
877 | 
878 |       // object.prototype.property = ...;
879 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
880 |         Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
881 |         String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
882 | 
883 |         if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
884 |           JSType jsType = getJSType(object2);
885 |           if (jsType.isFunctionType()) {
886 |             FunctionType functionType = jsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
887 |             if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
888 |               checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
889 |                   t, assign, functionType, property, info, getJSType(rvalue));
890 |             }
891 |           } else {
892 |             // TODO(user): might want to flag that
893 |           }
894 |           return;
895 |         }
896 |       }
897 | 
898 |       // object.property = ...;
899 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
900 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
901 |       if (type != null) {
902 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
903 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
904 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
905 |           validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
906 |               t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
907 |               type.getPropertyType(property), object, property);
908 |         }
909 |         return;
910 |       }
911 |     } else if (lvalue.isName()) {
912 |       // variable with inferred type case
913 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
914 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getString());
915 |       if (var != null) {
916 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
917 |           return;
918 |         }
919 |       }
920 |     }
921 | 
922 |     // fall through case
923 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
924 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
925 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
926 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
927 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {

===== M0266 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.isTry() &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0275 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1113-1137
Documentation:
/**
   * Suppose X is an object with inferred properties.
   * Suppose also that X is used in a way where it would only type-check
   * correctly if some of those properties are widened.
   * Then we should be polite and automatically widen X's properties for him.
   *
   * For a concrete example, consider:
   * param x {{prop: (number|undefined)}}
   * function f(x) {}
   * f({});
   *
   * If we give the anonymous object an inferred property of (number|undefined),
   * then this code will type-check appropriately.
   */
Method code:
1113 |   private void inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(
1114 |       JSType type, JSType constraint) {
1115 |     if (type == null || constraint == null) {
1116 |       return;
1117 |     }
1118 | 
1119 |     ObjectType constraintObj =
1120 |         ObjectType.cast(constraint.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1121 |     if (constraintObj != null && constraintObj.isRecordType()) {
1122 |       ObjectType objType = ObjectType.cast(type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1123 |       if (objType != null) {
1124 |         for (String prop : constraintObj.getOwnPropertyNames()) {
1125 |           JSType propType = constraintObj.getPropertyType(prop);
1126 |           if (!objType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(prop)) {
1127 |             JSType typeToInfer = propType;
1128 |             if (!objType.hasProperty(prop)) {
1129 |               typeToInfer =
1130 |                   getNativeType(VOID_TYPE).getLeastSupertype(propType);
1131 |             }
1132 |             objType.defineInferredProperty(prop, typeToInfer, null);
1133 |           }
1134 |         }
1135 |       }
1136 |     }
1137 |   }

===== M0283 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 459-474
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
459 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
460 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
461 |       Set<String> directives = null;
462 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
463 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
464 |         if (directives == null) {
465 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
466 |         } else {
467 |           directives.add(directive);
468 |         }
469 |       }
470 | 
471 |       if (directives != null) {
472 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
473 |       }
474 |     }

===== M0290 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 460-804
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
460 |   @Override
461 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
462 |     JSType childType;
463 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
464 |     Node left, right;
465 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
466 |     boolean typeable = true;
467 | 
468 |     switch (n.getType()) {
469 |       case Token.NAME:
470 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
471 |         break;
472 | 
473 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
474 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
475 |         // ignored here.
476 |         if (!parent.isFunction()) {
477 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
478 |         } else {
479 |           typeable = false;
480 |         }
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.COMMA:
484 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
485 |         break;
486 | 
487 |       case Token.TRUE:
488 |       case Token.FALSE:
489 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.THIS:
493 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
494 |         break;
495 | 
496 |       case Token.NULL:
497 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
498 |         break;
499 | 
500 |       case Token.NUMBER:
501 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
502 |         break;
503 | 
504 |       case Token.STRING:
505 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
506 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
507 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
508 |         } else {
509 |           // Object literal keys are not typeable
510 |           typeable = false;
511 |         }
512 |         break;
513 | 
514 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
515 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
516 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
517 |         break;
518 | 
519 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
520 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.REGEXP:
524 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
525 |         break;
526 | 
527 |       case Token.GETPROP:
528 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
529 |         typeable = !(parent.isAssign() &&
530 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.GETELEM:
534 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
535 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
536 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
537 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
538 |         typeable = false;
539 |         break;
540 | 
541 |       case Token.VAR:
542 |         visitVar(t, n);
543 |         typeable = false;
544 |         break;
545 | 
546 |       case Token.NEW:
547 |         visitNew(t, n);
548 |         typeable = true;
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.CALL:
552 |         visitCall(t, n);
553 |         typeable = !parent.isExprResult();
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.RETURN:
557 |         visitReturn(t, n);
558 |         typeable = false;
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.DEC:
562 |       case Token.INC:
563 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
564 |         validator.expectNumber(
565 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
566 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.NOT:
570 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
571 |         break;
572 | 
573 |       case Token.VOID:
574 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
575 |         break;
576 | 
577 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
578 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
579 |         break;
580 | 
581 |       case Token.BITNOT:
582 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
583 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
584 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
585 |               childType.toString());
586 |         }
587 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.POS:
591 |       case Token.NEG:
592 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
593 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
594 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
595 |         break;
596 | 
597 |       case Token.EQ:
598 |       case Token.NE: {
599 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
600 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
601 | 
602 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
603 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
604 |         TernaryValue result =
605 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
606 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
607 |           if (n.isNE()) {
608 |             result = result.not();
609 |           }
610 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
611 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
612 |         }
613 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
614 |         break;
615 |       }
616 | 
617 |       case Token.SHEQ:
618 |       case Token.SHNE: {
619 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
620 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
621 | 
622 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
623 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
624 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
625 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
626 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
627 |               rightType.toString());
628 |         }
629 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
630 |         break;
631 |       }
632 | 
633 |       case Token.LT:
634 |       case Token.LE:
635 |       case Token.GT:
636 |       case Token.GE:
637 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
638 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
639 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
640 |           validator.expectNumber(
641 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
642 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
643 |           validator.expectNumber(
644 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
645 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
646 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
647 |           // OK.
648 |         } else {
649 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
650 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
651 |           // should match a string context.
652 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
653 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
654 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
655 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
656 |           message = "right side of comparison";
657 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
658 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
659 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
660 |         }
661 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
662 |         break;
663 | 
664 |       case Token.IN:
665 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
666 |         right = n.getLastChild();
667 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
668 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
669 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
670 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
671 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
672 |         break;
673 | 
674 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
675 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
676 |         right = n.getLastChild();
677 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
678 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
679 | 
680 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
681 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
682 |         validator.expectActualObject(
683 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
684 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
685 |         break;
686 | 
687 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
688 |         visitAssign(t, n);
689 |         typeable = false;
690 |         break;
691 | 
692 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
693 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
694 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
695 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
696 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
697 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
698 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
699 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
700 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
701 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
702 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
703 |       case Token.LSH:
704 |       case Token.RSH:
705 |       case Token.URSH:
706 |       case Token.DIV:
707 |       case Token.MOD:
708 |       case Token.BITOR:
709 |       case Token.BITXOR:
710 |       case Token.BITAND:
711 |       case Token.SUB:
712 |       case Token.ADD:
713 |       case Token.MUL:
714 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
715 |         break;
716 | 
717 |       case Token.DELPROP:
718 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
719 |         break;
720 | 
721 |       case Token.CASE:
722 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
723 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
724 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
725 |         typeable = false;
726 |         break;
727 | 
728 |       case Token.WITH: {
729 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
730 |         childType = getJSType(child);
731 |         validator.expectObject(
732 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
733 |         typeable = false;
734 |         break;
735 |       }
736 | 
737 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
738 |         visitFunction(t, n);
739 |         break;
740 | 
741 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
742 |       case Token.LABEL:
743 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
744 |       case Token.SWITCH:
745 |       case Token.BREAK:
746 |       case Token.CATCH:
747 |       case Token.TRY:
748 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
749 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
750 |       case Token.BLOCK:
751 |       case Token.EMPTY:
752 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
753 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
754 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
755 |       case Token.THROW:
756 |         typeable = false;
757 |         break;
758 | 
759 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
760 |       case Token.DO:
761 |       case Token.FOR:
762 |       case Token.IF:
763 |       case Token.WHILE:
764 |         typeable = false;
765 |         break;
766 | 
767 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
768 |       case Token.AND:
769 |       case Token.HOOK:
770 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
771 |       case Token.OR:
772 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
773 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
774 |         } else {
775 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
776 |           if ((n.isObjectLit())
777 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
778 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
779 |           } else {
780 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
781 |           }
782 |         }
783 |         if (n.isObjectLit()) {
784 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {
785 |             visitObjLitKey(t, key, n);
786 |           }
787 |         }
788 |         break;
789 | 
790 |       default:
791 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
792 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
793 |         break;
794 |     }
795 | 
796 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
797 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
798 | 
799 |     if (typeable) {
800 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
801 |     }
802 | 
803 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
804 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3068-3083
Documentation:
/** Get the name of the given l-value node. */
Method code:
3068 |   static String getBestLValueName(@Nullable Node lValue) {
3069 |     if (lValue == null || lValue.getParent() == null) {
3070 |       return null;
3071 |     }
3072 |     if (isObjectLitKey(lValue, lValue.getParent())) {
3073 |       Node owner = getBestLValue(lValue.getParent());
3074 |       if (owner != null) {

3076 |         if (ownerName != null) {
3077 |           return ownerName + "." + getObjectLitKeyName(lValue);
3078 |         }
3079 |       }
3080 |       return null;
3081 |     }
3082 |     return lValue.getQualifiedName();
3083 |   }

===== M0296 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitGetProp(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1251-1277
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1251 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1252 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1253 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1254 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1255 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.isAssign()) {
1256 |       return;
1257 |     }
1258 | 
1259 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1260 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1261 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1262 |     // and what isn't.
1263 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1264 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1265 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1266 | 
1267 |     // TODO(user): remove in favor of flagging every property access on
1268 |     // non-object.
1269 |     if (!validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(t, n, childType,
1270 |             "No properties on this expression", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1271 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1272 |       return;
1273 |     }
1274 | 
1275 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1276 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1277 |   }

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1216-1227
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1216 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1217 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1218 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1219 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1220 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1221 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1222 |       } else {
1223 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1224 |       }
1225 |     }
1226 |     return builder.build();
1227 |   }

===== M0309 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 794-808
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
794 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
795 |     switch (n.getType()) {
796 |       case Token.DO:
797 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
798 |       case Token.FOR:
799 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
800 |           return n;
801 |         }
802 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
803 |       case Token.LABEL:
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
805 |       default:
806 |         return n;
807 |     }
808 |   }

===== M0334 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1545-1597
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1545 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1546 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1547 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1548 | 
1549 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1550 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1551 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1552 |       return;
1553 |     }
1554 | 
1555 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1556 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1557 |     if (childType.isFunctionType()) {
1558 |       FunctionType functionType = childType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1559 | 
1560 |       boolean isExtern = false;
1561 |       JSDocInfo functionJSDocInfo = functionType.getJSDocInfo();
1562 |       if( functionJSDocInfo != null  &&
1563 |           functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode() != null) {
1564 |         isExtern = functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode().isFromExterns();
1565 |       }
1566 | 
1567 |       // Non-native constructors should not be called directly
1568 |       // unless they specify a return type and are defined
1569 |       // in an extern.
1570 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1571 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType() &&
1572 |           (functionType.getReturnType().isUnknownType() ||
1573 |            functionType.getReturnType().isVoidType() ||
1574 |            !isExtern)) {
1575 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1576 |       }
1577 | 
1578 |       // Functions with explcit 'this' types must be called in a GETPROP
1579 |       // or GETELEM.
1580 |       if (functionType.isOrdinaryFunction() &&
1581 |           !functionType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType() &&
1582 |           !functionType.getTypeOfThis().isNativeObjectType() &&
1583 |           !(child.isGetElem() ||
1584 |             child.isGetProp())) {
1585 |         report(t, n, EXPECTED_THIS_TYPE, functionType.toString());
1586 |       }
1587 | 
1588 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1589 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1590 |     } else {
1591 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1592 |     }
1593 | 
1594 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1595 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1596 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.
1597 |   }

===== M0336 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 466-496
Documentation:

Method code:
466 |     @Override
467 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n,
468 |         Node parent) {
469 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
470 |       if (n.isFunction() ||
471 |           n.isScript()) {
472 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
473 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
474 |       }
475 | 
476 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
477 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
478 |       boolean descend = parent == null || !parent.isFunction() ||
479 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
480 | 
481 |       if (descend) {
482 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
483 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.
484 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementParent(n)) {
485 |           for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
486 |                child != null;
487 |                child = child.getNext()) {
488 |             if (NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(child)) {
489 |               defineFunctionLiteral(child, n);
490 |             }
491 |           }
492 |         }
493 |       }
494 | 
495 |       return descend;
496 |     }

/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
908 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
909 |     switch (n.getType()) {
910 |       case Token.CALL:
911 |       case Token.GETPROP:
912 |       case Token.GETELEM:
913 |       case Token.THROW:
914 |       case Token.NEW:
915 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
916 |       case Token.INC:
917 |       case Token.DEC:
918 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
919 |         return true;
920 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
921 |         return false;
922 |     }
923 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
924 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
925 |         return true;
926 |       }
927 |     }
928 |     return false;
929 |   }

===== M0340 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 498-555
Documentation:

Method code:
498 |     @Override
499 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
500 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
501 |       attachLiteralTypes(t, n);
502 | 
503 |       switch (n.getType()) {
504 |         case Token.CALL:
505 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
506 |           checkForCallingConventionDefiningCalls(n, delegateCallingConventions);
507 |           break;
508 | 
509 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
510 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
511 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
512 |           }
513 | 
514 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
515 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
516 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n, parent);
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 | 
520 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
521 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
522 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
523 |           if (firstChild.isGetProp() &&
524 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
525 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
526 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
527 |           }
528 |           break;
529 | 
530 |         case Token.CATCH:
531 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
532 |           break;
533 | 
534 |         case Token.VAR:
535 |           defineVar(n, parent);
536 |           break;
537 | 
538 |         case Token.GETPROP:
539 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
540 |           if (parent.isExprResult() &&
541 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
542 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
543 |           }
544 |           break;
545 |       }
546 | 
547 |       // Analyze any @lends object literals in this statement.
548 |       if (n.getParent() != null && NodeUtil.isStatement(n) &&
549 |           lentObjectLiterals != null) {
550 |         for (Node objLit : lentObjectLiterals) {
551 |           defineObjectLiteral(objLit);
552 |         }
553 |         lentObjectLiterals.clear();
554 |       }
555 |     }

356 |     } else if (prettyPrint) {
357 |       // Don't pretty print recursively.
358 |       prettyPrint = false;
359 | 
360 |       // Use a tree set so that the properties are sorted.
361 |       Set<String> propertyNames = Sets.newTreeSet();
362 |       for (ObjectType current = this;
363 |            current != null && !current.isNativeObjectType() &&
364 |                propertyNames.size() <= MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES;
365 |            current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
366 |         propertyNames.addAll(current.getOwnPropertyNames());
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
370 |       sb.append("{");
371 | 
372 |       int i = 0;
373 |       for (String property : propertyNames) {
374 |         if (i > 0) {
375 |           sb.append(", ");
376 |         }
377 | 
378 |         sb.append(property);
379 |         sb.append(": ");
380 |         sb.append(getPropertyType(property).toString());
381 | 
382 |         ++i;
383 |         if (i == MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES) {
384 |           sb.append(", ...");
385 |           break;
386 |         }
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       sb.append("}");
390 | 
391 |       prettyPrint = true;
392 |       return sb.toString();
393 |     } else {
394 |       return "{...}";
395 |     }
396 |   }

===== M0359 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 598-637
Documentation:

Method code:
598 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
599 |     String varName = n.getString();
600 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
601 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
602 |     if (value != null) {
603 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
604 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
605 |           getJSType(value));
606 |       return scope;
607 |     } else {
608 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
609 |       if (var != null) {
610 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
611 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
612 | 
613 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
614 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
615 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
616 |         boolean unflowable = isInferred &&
617 |             isUnflowable(syntacticScope.getVar(varName));
618 | 
619 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
620 |         // inferred variable.
621 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
622 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
623 |             isInferred &&
624 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
625 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
626 | 
627 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
628 |           type = var.getType();
629 |           if (type == null) {
630 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
631 |           }
632 |         }
633 |       }
634 |     }
635 |     n.setJSType(type);
636 |     return scope;
637 |   }

===== M0370 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 242-296
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
242 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
243 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
244 |     if (oldType == null) {
245 |       return this;
246 |     }
247 | 
248 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
249 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
250 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
251 |       // Not a function literal.
252 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
253 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
254 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
258 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
259 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
260 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
261 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
262 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
263 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
264 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
265 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
266 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
267 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
268 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
269 | 
270 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
271 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
272 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
273 | 
274 |           // The subclass method might write its var_args as individual
275 |           // arguments.
276 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
277 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
278 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
279 |           }
280 |         } else {
281 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
282 |               paramBuilder,
283 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
284 |               warnedAboutArgList,
285 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
286 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
287 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
288 |         }
289 |       }
290 | 
291 |       // Clone any remaining params that aren't in the function literal.
292 | 
293 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
294 |     }
295 |     return this;
296 |   }

===== M0385 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0399 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 945-1032
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function(this: T, ...) and T as parameters, type
   * inference will set the type of this on a function literal argument to the
   * the actual type of T.
   */
Method code:
945 |   private void updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
946 |     // TODO(user): Make the template logic more general.
947 | 
948 |     if (fnType.getTemplateTypeName() == null) {
949 |       return;
950 |     }
951 | 
952 |     int i = 0;
953 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
954 |     // Find the parameter whose type is the template type.
955 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
956 |       JSType iParameterType =
957 |           getJSType(iParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
958 |       if (iParameterType.isTemplateType()) {
959 |         // Find the actual type of this argument.
960 |         ObjectType iArgumentType = null;
961 |         if (i + 1 < childCount) {
962 |           Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
963 |           iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument)
964 |               .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined()
965 |               .collapseUnion()
966 |               .toObjectType();
967 |           if (iArgumentType == null) {
968 |             compiler.report(
969 |                 JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(iArgument), iArgument,
970 |                     TEMPLATE_TYPE_NOT_OBJECT_TYPE,
971 |                     getJSType(iArgument).toString()));
972 |             return;
973 |           }
974 |         }
975 | 
976 |         // Find the parameter whose type is function(this: T, ...)
977 |         boolean foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = false;
978 |         int j = 0;
979 |         for (Node jParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
980 |           JSType jParameterType =
981 |               getJSType(jParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
982 |           if (jParameterType.isFunctionType()) {
983 |             FunctionType jParameterFnType = jParameterType.toMaybeFunctionType();
984 |             if (jParameterFnType.getTypeOfThis().equals(iParameterType)) {
985 |               foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = true;
986 |               // Find the actual type of the this argument.
987 |               if (j + 1 >= childCount) {
988 |                 // TypeCheck#visitParameterList will warn so we bail.
989 |                 return;
990 |               }
991 |               Node jArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(j + 1);
992 |               JSType jArgumentType = getJSType(jArgument);
993 |               if (jArgument.isFunction() &&
994 |                   jArgumentType.isFunctionType()) {
995 |                 if (iArgumentType != null &&
996 |                     // null and undefined get filtered out above.
997 |                     !iArgumentType.isNoType()) {
998 |                   // If it's an function expression, update the type of this
999 |                   // using the actual type of T.
1000 |                   FunctionType jArgumentFnType = jArgumentType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1001 |                   if (jArgumentFnType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType()) {
1002 |                     // The new type will be picked up when we traverse the inner
1003 |                     // function.
1004 |                     jArgument.setJSType(
1005 |                         registry.createFunctionTypeWithNewThisType(
1006 |                             jArgumentFnType, iArgumentType));
1007 |                   }
1008 |                 } else {
1009 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal references this.
1010 |                   if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(
1011 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
1012 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
1013 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNDEFINED_THIS));
1014 |                   }
1015 |                 }
1016 |               }
1017 |               // TODO(user): Add code to TypeCheck to check that the
1018 |               // types of the arguments match.
1019 |             }
1020 |           }
1021 |           j++;
1022 |         }
1023 | 
1024 |         if (!foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter) {
1025 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
1026 |               TEMPLATE_TYPE_OF_THIS_EXPECTED));
1027 |           return;
1028 |         }
1029 |       }
1030 |       i++;
1031 |     }
1032 |   }

279 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
280 |     switch (c) {
281 |       case '@':
282 |       case '*':
283 |       case ',':
284 |       case '>':
285 |       case ':':

   */
Method code:
1704 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1705 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1706 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1707 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1708 |       skipEOLs();
1709 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1710 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1711 |       skipEOLs();
1712 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1713 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1714 |       skipEOLs();
1715 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1716 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1717 |       String string = stream.getString();
1718 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1719 |         skipEOLs();
1720 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1721 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1722 |         return newStringNode(string);
1723 |       } else {
1724 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1725 |       }
1726 |     }
1727 | 
1728 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1729 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1730 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 |     compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(acceptEs5);
126 | 
127 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
128 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
129 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 609-670
Documentation:

Method code:
609 |     @Override
610 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
611 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
612 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
613 |       if (name == null) {
614 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
615 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
616 |           errorReporter.error(
617 |             "unnamed function statement",
618 |             sourceName,
619 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
620 | 
621 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
622 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
623 |         }
624 |         name = new Name();
625 |         name.setIdentifier("");
626 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
627 |       }
628 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
629 |       Node newName = transform(name);
630 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
631 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
632 |         // declaration.
633 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
634 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
635 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
636 |         // same line as the function keyword.
637 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
638 |             functionNode.getLp();
639 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
640 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
641 |       }
642 | 
643 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
644 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
645 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
646 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
647 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
648 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
649 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
650 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
651 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
652 |       if (fnName != null) {
653 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
654 |       } else {
655 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
656 |       }
657 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
658 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
659 | 
660 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
661 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
662 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
663 |       }
664 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
665 | 
666 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
667 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
668 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
669 |      return node;
670 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 319-366
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
319 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
320 |     if (info != null) {
321 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
322 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
323 | 
324 |       // base type
325 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
326 |         if (isConstructor) {
327 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
328 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
329 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
330 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
331 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
332 |           }
333 |         } else {
334 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
335 |         }
336 |       }
337 | 
338 |       // implemented interfaces
339 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
340 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
341 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
342 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
343 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
344 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
345 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
346 |           }
347 |         }
348 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
349 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
350 |       }
351 | 
352 |       // extended interfaces (for interface only)
353 |       if (isInterface) {
354 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
355 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
356 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
357 |           if (maybeInterfaceType != null &&
358 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
359 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
360 |           }
361 |         }
362 |       }
363 |     }
364 | 
365 |     return this;
366 |   }

     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.

1636 |         Node n = stub.node;

1638 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1639 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1640 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1641 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1642 | 
1643 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1644 |           continue;

1659 |         } else {

1662 |         }

1103 |     // TODO(nicksantos): In ES3, the runtime coerces "null" to the global
1104 |     // activation object. In ES5, it leaves it as null. Just punt on this
1105 |     // issue for now by coercing out null. This is complicated by the

1122 |       resolvedInterfaces.add(resolvedIface);
1123 |       changed |= (resolvedIface != iface);
1124 |     }

3054 |   static Node getBestLValueOwner(@Nullable Node lValue) {
3055 |     if (lValue == null || lValue.getParent() == null) {
3056 |       return null;
3057 |     }
3058 |     if (isObjectLitKey(lValue, lValue.getParent())) {
3059 |       return getBestLValue(lValue.getParent());
3060 |     } else if (isGet(lValue)) {

3062 |     }
3063 | 
3064 |     return null;
3065 |   }

===== M0455 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 844-945
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the jsdoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
844 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
845 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
846 |         @Nullable String name,
847 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
848 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
849 | 
850 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
851 | 
852 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
853 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
854 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
855 |         if (var != null && var.getType() != null &&
856 |             var.getType().isFunctionType()) {
857 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = var.getType().toMaybeFunctionType();
858 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
859 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
860 |             functionType = aliasedType;
861 | 
862 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
863 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
864 |             }
865 |           }
866 |         }
867 |       }
868 | 
869 |       if (functionType == null) {
870 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
871 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
872 |             rValue != null && rValue.isFunction();
873 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
874 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
875 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
876 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
877 | 
878 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
879 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
880 | 
881 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
882 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
883 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
884 |             functionType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
885 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
886 |           }
887 |         }
888 | 
889 |         if (functionType == null) {
890 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
891 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(lvalueNode);
892 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
893 |           Var ownerVar = null;
894 |           String propName = null;
895 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
896 |           if (ownerName != null) {
897 |             ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
898 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
899 |               ownerType = ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType());
900 |             }
901 |             if (name != null) {
902 |               propName = name.substring(ownerName.length() + 1);
903 |             }
904 |           }
905 | 
906 |           FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
907 |           if (ownerType != null && propName != null) {
908 |             overriddenPropType =
909 |                 findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
910 |           }
911 | 
912 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
913 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
914 |                   scope)
915 |               .setContents(getFunctionAnalysisResults(fnRoot))
916 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
917 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
918 |               .inferReturnType(info)
919 |               .inferInheritance(info);
920 | 
921 | 
922 |           // Infer the context type.
923 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
924 |           if (ownerType != null && ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
925 |             builder.inferThisType(
926 |                 info, ownerType.getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType());
927 |             searchedForThisType = true;
928 |           } else if (ownerNode != null && ownerNode.isThis()) {
929 |             builder.inferThisType(info, ownerNode.getJSType());
930 |             searchedForThisType = true;
931 |           }
932 | 
933 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
934 |             builder.inferThisType(info);
935 |           }
936 | 
937 |           functionType = builder
938 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
939 |               .buildAndRegister();
940 |         }
941 |       }
942 | 
943 |       // all done
944 |       return functionType;
945 |     }

===== M0456 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 191-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
191 |   @Override
192 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
193 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
194 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
195 |     // show up in the type registry.
196 |     Scope newScope = null;
197 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
198 |     if (parent == null) {
199 |       // Run a first-order analysis over the syntax tree.
200 |       (new FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer(compiler, functionAnalysisResults))
201 |           .process(root.getFirstChild(), root.getLastChild());
202 | 
203 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
204 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
205 | 
206 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
207 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
208 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
209 |     } else {
210 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
211 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
212 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
213 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
214 |     }
215 | 
216 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
217 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
218 | 
219 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
220 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
221 |     // build properties on.
222 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
223 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
224 |       if (type != null && type.isFunctionType()) {
225 |         FunctionType fnType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
226 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
227 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
228 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
229 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
230 |         }
231 |       }
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     if (parent == null) {
235 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
236 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes,
237 |           delegateCallingConventions);
238 |     }
239 |     return newScope;
240 |   }

===== M0460 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2812-2827
Documentation:
/**
   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.
   */
Method code:
2812 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2813 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
2814 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2815 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2816 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2817 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2818 |       if (parent.isAssign()) {
2819 |         // on ASSIGNs
2820 |         fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2821 |       } else if (parent.isName()) {
2822 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2823 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2824 |       }
2825 |     }
2826 |     return fnInfo;
2827 |   }

===== M0472 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 811-835
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
811 |   @Override
812 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
813 |     FunctionType that =
814 |         JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(otherType);
815 |     if (that == null) {
816 |       return false;
817 |     }
818 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
819 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
820 |         return this == that;
821 |       }
822 |       return false;
823 |     }
824 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
825 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
826 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
827 |       }
828 |       return false;
829 |     }
830 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
831 |       return false;
832 |     }
833 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
834 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
835 |   }

===== M0489 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

/**

   * unrecognized string has to be bound to a NamedType object that will be
   * resolved later.

===== M0493 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1169-1177
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1169 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1170 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1171 |       return -1;
1172 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1173 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1174 |     } else {
1175 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1176 |     }
1177 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 288-311
Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

   *     flags

===== M0518 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

   * another {@link JSDocInfo} object.

82 |   TypeInference(AbstractCompiler compiler, ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg,

95 |     // type is VOID.
