249 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
250 |     switch (n.getType()) {
251 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
252 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
253 |         break;
254 | 
255 |       case Token.NAME:
256 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
257 |         break;
258 | 
259 |       case Token.GETPROP:
260 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
261 |         break;
262 | 
263 |       case Token.AND:
264 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
265 |             .createChildFlowScope();
266 |         break;
267 | 
268 |       case Token.OR:
269 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
270 |             .createChildFlowScope();
271 |         break;
272 | 
273 |       case Token.HOOK:
274 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
275 |         break;
276 | 
277 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
278 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
279 |         break;
280 | 
281 |       case Token.CALL:
282 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
283 |         break;
284 | 
285 |       case Token.NEW:
286 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
287 |         break;
288 | 
289 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
290 |       case Token.ADD:
291 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
292 |         break;
293 | 
294 |       case Token.POS:
295 |       case Token.NEG:
296 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
297 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
298 |         break;
299 | 
300 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
301 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
302 |         break;
303 | 
304 |       case Token.THIS:
305 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
306 |         break;
307 | 
308 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
309 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
310 |       case Token.LSH:
311 |       case Token.RSH:
312 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
313 |       case Token.URSH:
314 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
315 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
316 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
317 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
318 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
319 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
320 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
321 |       case Token.DIV:
322 |       case Token.MOD:
323 |       case Token.BITAND:
324 |       case Token.BITXOR:
325 |       case Token.BITOR:
326 |       case Token.MUL:
327 |       case Token.SUB:
328 |       case Token.DEC:
329 |       case Token.INC:
330 |       case Token.BITNOT:
331 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
332 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
333 |         break;
334 | 
335 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
336 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
337 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
338 |         break;
339 | 
340 |       case Token.COMMA:
341 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
342 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
343 |         break;
344 | 
345 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
346 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
347 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
348 |         break;
349 | 
350 |       case Token.DELPROP:
351 |       case Token.LT:
352 |       case Token.LE:
353 |       case Token.GT:
354 |       case Token.GE:
355 |       case Token.NOT:
356 |       case Token.EQ:
357 |       case Token.NE:
358 |       case Token.SHEQ:
359 |       case Token.SHNE:
360 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
361 |       case Token.IN:
362 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
363 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
364 |         break;
365 | 
366 |       case Token.GETELEM:
367 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
368 |         break;
369 | 
370 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
371 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
372 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isGetProp()) {
373 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
374 |         }
375 |         break;
376 | 
377 |       case Token.SWITCH:
378 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
379 |         break;
380 | 
381 |       case Token.RETURN:
382 |         scope = traverseReturn(n, scope);
383 |         break;
384 | 
385 |       case Token.VAR:
386 |       case Token.THROW:
387 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
388 |         break;
389 | 
390 |       case Token.CATCH:
391 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
392 |         break;
393 |     }
394 |     if (!n.isFunction()) {
395 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
396 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
397 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
398 | 
399 |         // A stubbed type cast on a qualified name should take
400 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
401 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
402 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&
403 |             n.getParent().isExprResult()) {
404 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
405 |         }
406 | 
407 |         n.setJSType(castType);
408 |       }
409 |     }
410 | 
411 |     return scope;
412 |   }

===== M0561 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 331-358
Documentation:

Method code:
331 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
332 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
333 |     // two cases:
334 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
335 |     // and
336 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
337 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
338 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
339 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
340 |     //
341 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
342 |     //
343 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
344 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
345 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
346 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
347 |     //
348 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
349 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
350 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
351 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
352 |         !(baseType instanceof PrototypeObjectType)) {
353 | 
354 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
355 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
356 |     }
357 |     setPrototype((PrototypeObjectType) baseType, propertyNode);
358 |   }

===== M0571 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 146-169
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
146 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that) {
147 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
148 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
149 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
150 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
151 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
152 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
153 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
154 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
155 |             !thisParamType.isEquivalentTo(otherParamType)) {
156 |           return false;
157 |         }
158 |       } else {
159 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
160 |           return false;
161 |         }
162 |       }
163 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
164 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
165 |     }
166 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
167 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
168 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
169 |   }

===== M0577 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.findOverriddenFunction(ObjectType,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 950-970
Documentation:
/**
     * Find the function that's being overridden on this type, if any.
     */
Method code:
950 |     private FunctionType findOverriddenFunction(
951 |         ObjectType ownerType, String propName) {
952 |       // First, check to see if the property is implemented
953 |       // on a superclass.
954 |       JSType propType = ownerType.getPropertyType(propName);
955 |       if (propType != null && propType.isFunctionType()) {
956 |         return propType.toMaybeFunctionType();
957 |       } else {
958 |         // If it's not, then check to see if it's implemented
959 |         // on an implemented interface.
960 |         for (ObjectType iface :
961 |                  ownerType.getCtorImplementedInterfaces()) {
962 |           propType = iface.getPropertyType(propName);
963 |           if (propType != null && propType.isFunctionType()) {
964 |             return propType.toMaybeFunctionType();
965 |           }
966 |         }
967 |       }
968 | 
969 |       return null;
970 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1452-1606
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1452 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1453 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1454 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1455 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1456 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1457 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1458 | 
1459 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1460 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1461 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1462 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1463 | 
1464 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1465 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1466 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1467 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1468 |         }
1469 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1470 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1471 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1472 | 
1473 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1474 |         return createOptionalType(
1475 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1476 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1477 | 
1478 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1479 |         return createOptionalType(
1480 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1481 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1482 | 
1483 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1484 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1485 | 
1486 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1487 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1488 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1489 | 
1490 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1491 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1492 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1493 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1494 |           builder.addAlternate(
1495 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1496 |         }
1497 |         return builder.build();
1498 | 
1499 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1500 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1501 | 
1502 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1503 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1504 | 
1505 |       case Token.STRING:
1506 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1507 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1508 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1509 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1510 |         }
1511 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1512 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1513 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1514 |           if (typeList != null &&
1515 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1516 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1517 |             JSType parameterType =
1518 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1519 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1520 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1521 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1522 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1523 |               JSType indexType =
1524 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1525 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1526 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1527 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1528 |             }
1529 |           }
1530 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1531 |         } else {
1532 |           return namedType;
1533 |         }
1534 | 
1535 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1536 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1537 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1538 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1539 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1540 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1541 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1542 |           thisType =
1543 |               ObjectType.cast(
1544 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1545 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1546 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1547 |           if (thisType == null) {
1548 |             reporter.warning(
1549 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1550 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1551 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1552 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1553 |                 sourceName,
1554 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), contextNode.getCharno());
1555 |           }
1556 | 
1557 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1558 |           current = current.getNext();
1559 |         }
1560 | 
1561 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1562 | 
1563 |         if (current.getType() == Token.PARAM_LIST) {
1564 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1565 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1566 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1567 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1568 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1569 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1570 |               } else {
1571 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1572 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1573 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1574 |               }
1575 |             } else {
1576 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1577 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1578 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1579 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1580 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1581 |                   reporter.warning(
1582 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1583 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), arg.getCharno());
1584 |                 }
1585 |               } else {
1586 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1587 |               }
1588 |             }
1589 |           }
1590 |           current = current.getNext();
1591 |         }
1592 | 
1593 |         JSType returnType =
1594 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1595 | 
1596 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1597 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1598 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1599 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1600 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1601 |             .build();
1602 |     }
1603 | 
1604 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1605 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1606 |   }

===== M0614 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0627 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.getReadableJSTypeName(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 685-735
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a node, get a human-readable name for the type of that node so
   * that will be easy for the programmer to find the original declaration.
   *
   * For example, if SubFoo's property "bar" might have the human-readable
   * name "Foo.prototype.bar".
   *
   * @param n The node.
   * @param dereference If true, the type of the node will be dereferenced
   *     to an Object type, if possible.
   */
Method code:
685 |   String getReadableJSTypeName(Node n, boolean dereference) {
686 |     // If we're analyzing a GETPROP, the property may be inherited by the
687 |     // prototype chain. So climb the prototype chain and find out where
688 |     // the property was originally defined.
689 |     if (n.isGetProp()) {
690 |       ObjectType objectType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild()).dereference();
691 |       if (objectType != null) {
692 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
693 |         if (objectType.getConstructor() != null &&
694 |             objectType.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
695 |           objectType = FunctionType.getTopDefiningInterface(
696 |               objectType, propName);
697 |         } else {
698 |           // classes
699 |           while (objectType != null && !objectType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
700 |             objectType = objectType.getImplicitPrototype();
701 |           }
702 |         }
703 | 
704 |         // Don't show complex function names or anonymous types.
705 |         // Instead, try to get a human-readable type name.
706 |         if (objectType != null &&
707 |             (objectType.getConstructor() != null ||
708 |              objectType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
709 |           return objectType.toString() + "." + propName;
710 |         }
711 |       }
712 |     }
713 | 
714 |     JSType type = getJSType(n);
715 |     if (dereference) {
716 |       ObjectType dereferenced = type.dereference();
717 |       if (dereferenced != null) {
718 |         type = dereferenced;
719 |       }
720 |     }
721 | 
722 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
723 |     if (type.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
724 |         (type.toObjectType() != null &&
725 |          type.toObjectType().getConstructor() != null)) {
726 |       return type.toString();
727 |     } else if (qualifiedName != null) {
728 |       return qualifiedName;
729 |     } else if (type.isFunctionType()) {
730 |       // Don't show complex function names.
731 |       return "function";
732 |     } else {
733 |       return type.toString();
734 |     }
735 |   }

===== M0641 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock());
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0643 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseNew(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1034-1063
Documentation:

Method code:
1034 |   private FlowScope traverseNew(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1035 |     Node constructor = n.getFirstChild();
1036 |     scope = traverse(constructor, scope);
1037 | 
1038 |     JSType constructorType = constructor.getJSType();
1039 |     JSType type = null;
1040 |     if (constructorType != null) {
1041 |       constructorType = constructorType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1042 |       if (constructorType.isUnknownType()) {
1043 |         type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1044 |       } else {
1045 |         FunctionType ct = constructorType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1046 |         if (ct == null && constructorType instanceof FunctionType) {
1047 |           // If constructorType is a NoObjectType, then toMaybeFunctionType will
1048 |           // return null. But NoObjectType implements the FunctionType
1049 |           // interface, precisely because it can validly construct objects.
1050 |           ct = (FunctionType) constructorType;
1051 |         }
1052 |         if (ct != null && ct.isConstructor()) {
1053 |           type = ct.getInstanceType();
1054 |         }
1055 |       }
1056 |     }
1057 |     n.setJSType(type);
1058 | 
1059 |     for (Node arg = constructor.getNext(); arg != null; arg = arg.getNext()) {
1060 |       scope = traverse(arg, scope);
1061 |     }
1062 |     return scope;
1063 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1126-1148
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor or an interface type, find out whether the unknown
   * type is a supertype of the current type.
   */
Method code:
1126 |   private static boolean hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType ctor) {
1127 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1128 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(!ctor.isUnknownType());
1129 | 
1130 |     // The type system should notice inheritance cycles on its own
1131 |     // and break the cycle.
1132 |     while (true) {
1133 |       ObjectType maybeSuperInstanceType =
1134 |           ctor.getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype();
1135 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType == null) {
1136 |         return false;
1137 |       }
1138 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType.isUnknownType() ||
1139 |           maybeSuperInstanceType.isEmptyType()) {
1140 |         return true;
1141 |       }
1142 |       ctor = maybeSuperInstanceType.getConstructor();
1143 |       if (ctor == null) {
1144 |         return false;
1145 |       }
1146 |       Preconditions.checkState(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1147 |     }
1148 |   }

===== M0670 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 128-179
Documentation:

Method code:
128 |     @Override
129 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
130 |       // null is the smallest value
131 |       if (p2 == null) {
132 |         if (p1 == null) {
133 |           return 0;
134 |         } else {
135 |           return P1_GT_P2;
136 |         }
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       // check level
140 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
141 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
142 |       }
143 | 
144 |       // sourceName comparison
145 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
146 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
147 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
148 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
149 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
150 |           return sourceCompare;
151 |         }
152 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
153 |         return P1_LT_P2;
154 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
155 |         return P1_GT_P2;
156 |       }
157 |       // lineno comparison
158 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
159 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
160 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
161 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
162 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
163 |         return P1_LT_P2;
164 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
165 |         return P1_GT_P2;
166 |       }
167 |       // charno comparison
168 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
169 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
170 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
171 |         return charno1 - charno2;
172 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
173 |         return P1_LT_P2;
174 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
175 |         return P1_GT_P2;
176 |       }
177 |       // description
178 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
179 |     }

/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
595 |   public Scope getScope() {
596 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
597 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
598 |       return scope;
599 |     }
600 | 
601 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
602 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
603 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
604 |       scopes.push(scope);
605 |     }
606 |     scopeRoots.clear();
607 | 
608 |     return scope;
609 |   }

===== M0698 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,CompilerInput,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 501-561
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   * @return The variable we end up with. Most of the time, this will just
   *     be {@code var}, but in some rare cases we will need to declare
   *     a new var with new source info.
   */
Method code:
501 |   Var expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, CompilerInput input,
502 |       Node n, Node parent, Var var, String variableName, JSType newType) {
503 |     Var newVar = var;
504 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
505 |     if (n.isGetProp() ||
506 |         NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
507 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
508 |       if (info == null) {
509 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
510 |       }
511 |       allowDupe =
512 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
513 |     }
514 | 
515 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
516 | 
517 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
518 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
519 |     // compilation process.
520 |     if (varType != null &&
521 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
522 |         newType != null &&
523 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
524 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
525 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
526 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
527 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
528 |       // native type. We should redeclare it at the new input site.
529 |       if (var.input == null) {
530 |         Scope s = var.getScope();
531 |         s.undeclare(var);
532 |         newVar = s.declare(variableName, n, varType, input, false);
533 | 
534 |         n.setJSType(varType);
535 |         if (parent.isVar()) {
536 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
537 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
538 |           }
539 |         } else {
540 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.isFunction());
541 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
542 |         }
543 |       } else {
544 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
545 |         // match the original type.
546 |         //
547 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
548 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
549 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
550 |               var.getParentNode().isExprResult()) ||
551 |             !newType.equals(varType)) {
552 |           report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
553 |               variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
554 |               String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
555 |               varType.toString()));
556 |         }
557 |       }
558 |     }
559 | 
560 |     return newVar;
561 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.getTopMostDefiningType(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 787-805
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor or an interface type and a property, finds the
   * top-most superclass that has the property defined (including this
   * constructor).
   */
Method code:
787 |   public ObjectType getTopMostDefiningType(String propertyName) {
788 |     Preconditions.checkState(isConstructor() || isInterface());
789 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName));
790 |     FunctionType ctor = this;
791 | 
792 |     if (isInterface()) {
793 |       return getTopDefiningInterface(this.getInstanceType(), propertyName);
794 |     }
795 | 
796 |     ObjectType topInstanceType = ctor.getInstanceType();
797 |     while (true) {
798 |       topInstanceType = ctor.getInstanceType();
799 |       ctor = ctor.getSuperClassConstructor();
800 |       if (ctor == null || !ctor.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName)) {
801 |         break;
802 |       }
803 |     }
804 |     return topInstanceType;
805 |   }

===== M0724 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

/**
   * When "bind" is called on a function, we infer the type of the returned
   * "bound" function by looking at the number of parameters in the call site.
   */

889 |   private void updateBind(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
890 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Use the coding convention, so that we get goog.bind
891 |     // for free.

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2318-2334
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2318 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2319 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2320 |     int c;
2321 |     while (true) {
2322 |       c = stream.getChar();
2323 |       if (c == ' ') {
2324 |         continue;
2325 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2326 |         matchedLc = true;
2327 |         break;
2328 |       } else {
2329 |         break;
2330 |       }
2331 |     }
2332 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2333 |     return matchedLc;
2334 |   }

===== M0741 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredType(String,JSDocInfo,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1262-1310
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1262 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(String sourceName, JSDocInfo info,
1263 |         Node lValue, @Nullable Node rValue) {
1264 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1265 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1266 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.isFunction() &&
1267 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1268 |               JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(rValue.getJSType()), info, lValue)) {
1269 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1270 |       } else if (info != null) {
1271 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1272 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.isObjectLit()) {
1273 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1274 |           } else {
1275 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1276 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1277 |           }
1278 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1279 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1280 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1281 |         } else {
1282 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1283 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1284 |             JSType knownType = null;
1285 |             if (rValue != null) {
1286 |               if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1287 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1288 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1289 |               } else if (rValue.isOr()) {
1290 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1291 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1292 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1293 |                 // reasons.
1294 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1295 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1296 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.isName()
1297 |                     && lValue.isName()
1298 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1299 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1300 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1301 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1302 |                 }
1303 |               }
1304 |             }
1305 |           }
1306 |         }
1307 |       }
1308 | 
1309 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1310 |     }

97 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
98 |       case Token.NAME:
99 |         if (parent == null) {
100 |           return;
101 |         }
102 | 
103 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
104 |         if (!parent.isVar() &&
105 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
106 |             !(parent.isAssign() &&
107 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
108 |           return;
109 |         }
110 | 
111 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
112 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
113 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
114 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
115 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
116 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
117 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
118 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
119 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
120 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
121 |         if (docInfo == null &&
122 |             !(parent.isVar() &&
123 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
124 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
125 |         }
126 | 
127 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
128 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
129 |         if (varType == null && parent.isFunction()) {
130 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
134 |         // we can do.
135 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
136 |           return;
137 |         }
138 | 
139 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
140 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
141 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
142 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
143 |           return;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
147 |         break;
148 | 
149 |       case Token.GETPROP:
150 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
151 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
152 |         //
153 |         // 1)
154 |         // /** @deprecated */
155 |         // obj.prop = ...
156 |         //
157 |         // 2)
158 |         // /** @deprecated */
159 |         // obj.prop;
160 |         if (parent.isExprResult() ||
161 |             (parent.isAssign() &&
162 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
163 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
164 |           if (docInfo == null) {
165 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
166 |           }
167 |           if (docInfo != null) {
168 |             ObjectType lhsType =
169 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
170 |             if (lhsType != null) {
171 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
172 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
173 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
174 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo);
175 |               }
176 | 
177 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
178 |               ObjectType propType =
179 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
180 |               if (propType != null) {
181 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
182 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
183 |               }
184 |             }
185 |           }
186 |         }
187 |         break;
188 |     }
189 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3003-3029
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3003 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3004 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3005 |     if (info == null) {
3006 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3007 |       if (parent == null) {
3008 |         return null;
3009 |       }
3010 | 
3011 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3012 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3013 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3014 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3015 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3016 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3017 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3018 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3019 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3020 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3021 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3022 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3023 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3024 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3025 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3026 |       }
3027 |     }
3028 |     return info;
3029 |   }

===== M0754 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1090 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1091 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1092 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1093 | 
1094 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.isName() && scope.isGlobal();
1095 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1096 |           isGlobalVar &&
1097 |           (parent.isVar() ||
1098 |            parent.isFunction());
1099 | 
1100 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1101 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1102 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1103 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1104 |       if (n.isGetProp() && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1105 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1106 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1107 | 
1108 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1109 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1110 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1111 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1112 |         }
1113 |       }
1114 | 
1115 |       // The input may be null if we are working with a AST snippet. So read
1116 |       // the extern info from the node.
1117 |       boolean isExtern = n.isFromExterns();
1118 |       Var newVar = null;
1119 | 
1120 |       // declared in closest scope?
1121 |       CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(inputId);
1122 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1123 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1124 |         newVar = validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1125 |             sourceName, input, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1126 |       } else {
1127 |         if (!inferred) {
1128 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1129 |         }
1130 | 
1131 |         newVar =
1132 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1133 | 
1134 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1135 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1136 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1137 |               (initialValue.isObjectLit() ||
1138 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1139 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1140 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1141 |           }
1142 |         }
1143 |       }
1144 | 
1145 |       // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1146 |       FunctionType fnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(type);
1147 |       if (fnType != null &&
1148 |           // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1149 |           !type.isEmptyType()) {
1150 |         if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1151 |             !fnType.equals(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1152 |           // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1153 |           FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1154 |           ObjectType.Property prototypeSlot = fnType.getSlot("prototype");
1155 | 
1156 |           // When we declare the function prototype implicitly, we
1157 |           // want to make sure that the function and its prototype
1158 |           // are declared at the same node. We also want to make sure
1159 |           // that the if a symbol has both a Var and a JSType, they have
1160 |           // the same node.
1161 |           //
1162 |           // This consistency is helpful to users of SymbolTable,
1163 |           // because everything gets declared at the same place.
1164 |           prototypeSlot.setNode(n);
1165 | 
1166 |           String prototypeName = variableName + ".prototype";
1167 | 
1168 |           // There are some rare cases where the prototype will already
1169 |           // be declared. See TypedScopeCreatorTest#testBogusPrototypeInit.
1170 |           // Fortunately, other warnings will complain if this happens.
1171 |           Var prototypeVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(prototypeName);
1172 |           if (prototypeVar != null && prototypeVar.scope == scopeToDeclareIn) {
1173 |             scopeToDeclareIn.undeclare(prototypeVar);
1174 |           }
1175 | 
1176 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(prototypeName,
1177 |               n, prototypeSlot.getType(), input,
1178 |               /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1179 |               superClassCtor == null ||
1180 |               superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1181 |                   getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1182 | 
1183 |           // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1184 |           // it constructs itself.
1185 |           if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1186 |               !isExtern &&
1187 |               // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1188 |               // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1189 |               // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1190 |               // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1191 |               // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1192 |               // this.
1193 |               variableName.equals(
1194 |                   fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1195 |             compiler.report(
1196 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1197 |                     fnType.isConstructor() ?
1198 |                     CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1199 |                     variableName));
1200 |           }
1201 |         }
1202 |       }
1203 | 
1204 |       if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1205 |         ObjectType globalThis =
1206 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1207 |         if (inferred) {
1208 |           globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1209 |               type == null ?
1210 |               getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1211 |               type,
1212 |               n);
1213 |         } else {
1214 |           globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, n);
1215 |         }
1216 |       }
1217 | 
1218 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1219 |           && type != null
1220 |           && type.isFunctionType()
1221 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1222 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1223 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1224 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1225 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();

1228 |       }

1154 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1155 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1156 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1157 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1158 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1159 |     if (var != null) {
1160 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1161 |       if (varType != null) {
1162 |         if (varType.equals(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) &&
1163 |             var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName)) {
1164 |           // If the type of this qualified name has been checked in this scope,
1165 |           // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1166 |           return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1167 |         } else {
1168 |           return varType;
1169 |         }
1170 |       }
1171 |     }
1172 | 
1173 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1174 |     if (objType != null) {
1175 |       propertyType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1176 |     }
1177 | 
1178 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType()) &&
1179 |         qualifiedName != null) {

1181 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1182 |       if (regType != null) {

1185 |     }
1186 |

1188 |   }

===== M0779 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.isQualifiedNameInferred(String,Node,JSDocInfo,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1588-1613
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a qualified name is inferred.
     * NOTE(nicksantos): Determining whether a property is declared or not
     * is really really obnoxious.
     *
     * The problem is that there are two (equally valid) coding styles:
     *
     * (function() {
     *   /* The authoritative definition of goog.bar. /
     *   goog.bar = function() {};
     * })();
     *
     * function f() {
     *   goog.bar();
     *   /* Reset goog.bar to a no-op. /
     *   goog.bar = function() {};
     * }
     *
     * In a dynamic language with first-class functions, it's very difficult
     * to know which one the user intended without looking at lots of
     * contextual information (the second example demonstrates a small case
     * of this, but there are some really pathological cases as well).
     *
     * The current algorithm checks if either the declaration has
     * jsdoc type information, or @const with a known type,
     * or a function literal with a name we haven't seen before.
     */
Method code:
1588 |     private boolean isQualifiedNameInferred(
1589 |         String qName, Node n, JSDocInfo info,
1590 |         Node rhsValue, JSType valueType) {
1591 |       if (valueType == null) {
1592 |         return true;
1593 |       }
1594 | 
1595 |       boolean inferred = true;
1596 |       if (info != null) {
1597 |         inferred = !(info.hasType()
1598 |             || info.hasEnumParameterType()
1599 |             || (info.isConstant() && valueType != null
1600 |                 && !valueType.isUnknownType())
1601 |             || FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1602 |       }
1603 | 
1604 |       if (inferred && rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isFunction()) {
1605 |         if (info != null) {
1606 |           inferred = false;
1607 |         } else if (!scope.isDeclared(qName, false) &&
1608 |                    n.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
1609 |           inferred = false;
1610 |         }
1611 |       }
1612 |       return inferred;
1613 |     }

===== M0781 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1475-1536
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1475 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1476 |     FunctionType functionType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(n.getJSType());
1477 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1478 |     if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1479 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1480 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1481 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1482 |           (baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1483 |         compiler.report(
1484 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1485 |       } else {
1486 |         // All interfaces are properly implemented by a class
1487 |         for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1488 |           boolean badImplementedType = false;
1489 |           ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1490 |           if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1491 |             FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1492 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1493 |             if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1494 |                 !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1495 |               badImplementedType = true;
1496 |             }
1497 |           } else {
1498 |             badImplementedType = true;
1499 |           }
1500 |           if (badImplementedType) {
1501 |             report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1502 |           }
1503 |         }
1504 |         // check properties
1505 |         validator.expectAllInterfaceProperties(t, n, functionType);
1506 |       }
1507 |     } else if (functionType.isInterface()) {
1508 |       // Interface must extend only interfaces
1509 |       for (ObjectType extInterface : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1510 |         if (extInterface.getConstructor() != null
1511 |             && !extInterface.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
1512 |           compiler.report(
1513 |               t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1514 |         }
1515 |       }
1516 |       // Interface cannot implement any interfaces
1517 |       if (functionType.hasImplementedInterfaces()) {
1518 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n,
1519 |             CONFLICTING_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1520 |       }
1521 |       // Check whether the extended interfaces have any conflicts
1522 |       if (functionType.getExtendedInterfacesCount() > 1) {
1523 |         // Only check when extending more than one interfaces
1524 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> properties
1525 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1526 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> currentProperties
1527 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1528 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1529 |           currentProperties.clear();
1530 |           checkInterfaceConflictProperties(t, n, functionPrivateName,
1531 |               properties, currentProperties, interfaceType);
1532 |           properties.putAll(currentProperties);
1533 |         }
1534 |       }
1535 |     }
1536 |   }

/**
     * If a variable is assigned a function literal in the global scope,
     * make that a declared type (even if there's no doc info).
     * There's only one exception to this rule:
     * if the return type is inferred, and we're in a local
     * scope, we should assume the whole function is inferred.
     */
Method code:
810 |     private boolean shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
811 |         FunctionType type, JSDocInfo info, Node lValue) {
812 |       if (info != null) {
813 |         return true;
814 |       }
815 |       if (lValue != null &&
816 |           NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(lValue, lValue.getParent())) {
817 |         return false;
818 |       }
819 |       return scope.isGlobal() || !type.isReturnTypeInferred();
820 |     }

===== M0793 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)

Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0804 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitParameterList(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1602-1636
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1602 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1603 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1604 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1605 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1606 | 
1607 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1608 |     int ordinal = 0;
1609 |     Node parameter = null;
1610 |     Node argument = null;
1611 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1612 |            (parameters.hasNext() ||
1613 |             parameter != null && parameter.isVarArgs())) {
1614 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1615 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1616 |       if (parameters.hasNext()) {
1617 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1618 |       }
1619 |       argument = arguments.next();
1620 |       ordinal++;
1621 | 
1622 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1623 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1624 |     }
1625 | 
1626 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1627 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1628 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1629 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1630 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1631 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1632 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1633 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1634 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1635 |     }
1636 |   }

===== M0805 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1212-1241
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1212 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1213 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1214 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1215 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1216 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1217 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1218 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1219 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1220 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1221 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1222 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1223 |         parentNodeType == Token.PARAM_LIST ||
1224 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1225 |       return false;
1226 |     }
1227 | 
1228 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1229 |     if (type == null) {
1230 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1231 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1232 |       if (var != null) {
1233 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1234 |         if (varType != null) {
1235 |           type = varType;
1236 |         }
1237 |       }
1238 |     }
1239 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1240 |     return true;
1241 |   }

===== M0806 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 651-687
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
651 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
652 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
653 |         fnName, contents.getSourceNode(), parametersNode, returnType);
654 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
655 | 
656 |     if (existingType != null) {
657 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType.isInstanceType();
658 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
659 |         FunctionType existingFn =
660 |             isInstanceObject ?
661 |             existingType.toObjectType().getConstructor() :
662 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
663 | 
664 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
665 |           existingFn.setSource(contents.getSourceNode());
666 |         }
667 | 
668 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
669 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
670 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
671 |         }
672 | 
673 |         return existingFn;
674 |       } else {
675 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
676 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
677 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
678 |       }
679 |     }
680 | 
681 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
682 | 
683 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
684 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
685 |     }
686 |     return fnType;
687 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 136-157
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
136 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
137 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
138 |     String methodName = null;
139 |     if (callName.isGetProp()) {
140 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
141 |     } else if (callName.isName()) {
142 |       String name = callName.getString();
143 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
144 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
145 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
146 |       }
147 |     }
148 | 
149 |     if (methodName != null) {
150 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
151 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
152 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
153 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
154 |       }
155 |     }
156 |     return null;
157 |   }

===== M0820 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfParameters(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 913-938
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function parameters, type inference will set the type of
   * a function literal argument from the function parameter type.
   */
Method code:
913 |   private void updateTypeOfParameters(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
914 |     int i = 0;
915 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
916 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
917 |       if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
918 |         // TypeCheck#visitParametersList will warn so we bail.
919 |         return;
920 |       }
921 | 
922 |       JSType iParameterType = getJSType(iParameter);
923 |       Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
924 |       JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
925 |       inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(iArgumentType, iParameterType);
926 | 
927 |       if (iParameterType.isFunctionType()) {
928 |         FunctionType iParameterFnType = iParameterType.toMaybeFunctionType();
929 | 
930 |         if (iArgument.isFunction() &&
931 |             iArgumentType.isFunctionType() &&
932 |             iArgument.getJSDocInfo() == null) {
933 |           iArgument.setJSType(iParameterFnType);
934 |         }
935 |       }
936 |       i++;
937 |     }
938 |   }

===== M0829 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1902-1904
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1902 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1903 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1904 |   }

===== M0831 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)

Original range: 309-316
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
309 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
310 |     if (c <= 127) {
311 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
312 |     } else {
313 |       return c == 0xA0
314 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
315 |     }

/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

===== M0834 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 463-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
463 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
464 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
465 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
466 |     switch (left.getType()) {
467 |       case Token.NAME:
468 |         String varName = left.getString();
469 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
470 | 
471 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
472 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
473 |         // This has two purposes:
474 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
475 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
476 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
477 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
478 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
479 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
480 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
481 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
482 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
483 |           redeclareSimpleVar(scope, left, resultType);
484 |         }
485 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
486 |             resultType : null);
487 | 
488 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
489 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
490 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
491 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
492 |         }
493 |         break;
494 |       case Token.GETPROP:
495 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
496 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
497 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(left, qualifiedName,
498 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
499 |               resultType);
500 |         }
501 | 
502 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
503 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
504 |         break;
505 |     }
506 |   }
