392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1737-1772
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1737 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1738 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1739 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1740 |     }
1741 | 
1742 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1743 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1744 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1745 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1746 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1747 |       skipEOLs();
1748 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1749 |         next();
1750 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1751 |       }
1752 |     }
1753 | 
1754 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1755 | 
1756 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1757 |       next();
1758 |       skipEOLs();
1759 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1760 |       if (memberType != null) {
1761 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1762 | 
1763 |         skipEOLs();
1764 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1765 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1766 |         }
1767 | 
1768 |         next();
1769 |       }
1770 |     }
1771 |     return typeNameNode;
1772 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 799-841
Documentation:

Method code:
799 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
800 |       Node callNode) {
801 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
802 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
803 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
804 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
805 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
806 |       return scope;
807 |     }
808 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
809 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
810 |       return scope;
811 |     }
812 |     JSTypeNative assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType();
813 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
814 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
815 |     if (assertedType == null) {
816 |       if (assertedNodeName != null) {
817 |         JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
818 |         JSType narrowed = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
819 |         if (type != narrowed) {
820 |           scope = narrowScope(scope, assertedNode, narrowed);
821 |           callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
822 |         }
823 |       } else if (assertedNode.isAnd() ||
824 |                  assertedNode.isOr()) {
825 |         BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes =
826 |             traverseWithinShortCircuitingBinOp(assertedNode, scope);
827 |         scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
828 |             assertedNode, conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
829 |                 assertedNode.getType(), true), true);
830 |       }
831 |     } else if (assertedNodeName != null) {
832 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
833 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
834 |       JSType narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(getNativeType(assertedType));
835 |       if (type != narrowed) {
836 |         scope = narrowScope(scope, assertedNode, narrowed);
837 |         callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
838 |       }
839 |     }
840 |     return scope;
841 |   }

===== M0878 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 151-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
151 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
152 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
153 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
154 |       case Token.BLOCK:
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |       case Token.TRY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

/**
   * <p>Checks enum aliases.
   *
   * <p>We verify that the enum element type of the enum used
   * for initialization is a subtype of the enum element type of
   * the enum the value is being copied in.</p>
   *
   * <p>Example:</p>
   * <pre>var myEnum = myOtherEnum;</pre>

===== M0892 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.backwardsInferenceFromCallSite(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 879-883
Documentation:
/**
   * We only do forward type inference. We do not do full backwards
   * type inference.
   *
   * In other words, if we have,
   * <code>
   * var x = f();
   * g(x);
   * </code>
   * a forward type-inference engine would try to figure out the type
   * of "x" from the return type of "f". A backwards type-inference engine
   * would try to figure out the type of "x" from the parameter type of "g".
   *
   * However, there are a few special syntactic forms where we do some
   * some half-assed backwards type-inference, because programmers
   * expect it in this day and age. To take an example from java,
   * <code>
   * List<String> x = Lists.newArrayList();
   * </code>
   * The Java compiler will be able to infer the generic type of the List
   * returned by newArrayList().
   *
   * In much the same way, we do some special-case backwards inference for
   * JS. Those cases are enumerated here.
   */
Method code:
879 |   private void backwardsInferenceFromCallSite(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
880 |     updateTypeOfParameters(n, fnType);
881 |     updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(n, fnType);
882 |     updateBind(n, fnType);
883 |   }

===== M0893 =====

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 333-336
Documentation:

Method code:
333 |   private void declareNativeValueType(Scope scope, String name,

/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1105 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {
1106 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1107 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1108 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno();
1109 | 
1110 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1111 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1112 |       int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1113 |       int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1114 | 
1115 |       jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno,
1116 |           endLineno, endCharno, true);
1117 |     }
1118 |     return typeNode;
1119 |   }

===== M0909 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 930-972
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
930 |   @Override
931 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
932 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
933 |       return true;
934 |     }
935 | 
936 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
937 |       FunctionType other = that.toMaybeFunctionType();
938 |       if (other.isInterface()) {
939 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
940 |         return true;
941 |       }
942 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
943 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
944 |         return false;
945 |       }
946 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
947 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
948 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
949 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
950 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
951 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
952 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
953 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
954 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
955 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
956 | 
957 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
958 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
959 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
960 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
961 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
962 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
963 | 
964 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
965 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
966 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
967 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
968 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
969 |     }
970 | 
971 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
972 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

Method code:
773 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {

===== M0927 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
190 |       case Token.COLON:
191 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
192 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
193 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
194 |       case Token.DO:
195 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
196 |       case Token.EMPTY:
197 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
198 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
199 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
200 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
201 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
202 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
203 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
204 |         } else {
205 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
206 |                                           node.getClass() +
207 |                                           " type: " +
208 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
209 |         }
210 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
211 |       case Token.FALSE:
212 |       case Token.NULL:
213 |       case Token.THIS:
214 |       case Token.TRUE:
215 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
216 |       case Token.FOR:
217 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
218 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
219 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
220 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
221 |         } else {
222 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
223 |                                           node.getClass() +
224 |                                           " type: " +
225 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
226 |         }
227 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
228 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
229 |       case Token.GETELEM:
230 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
231 |       case Token.GETPROP:
232 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
233 |       case Token.HOOK:
234 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
235 |       case Token.IF:
236 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
237 |       case Token.LABEL:
238 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
239 |       case Token.LP:
240 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
241 |       case Token.NAME:
242 |         return processName((Name) node);
243 |       case Token.NEW:
244 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NUMBER:
246 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
247 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
248 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
249 |       case Token.REGEXP:
250 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.RETURN:
252 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
253 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
254 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
255 |       case Token.STRING:
256 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
257 |       case Token.SWITCH:
258 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
259 |       case Token.THROW:
260 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
261 |       case Token.TRY:
262 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.CONST:
264 |       case Token.VAR:
265 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
266 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
267 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
268 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
269 |         } else {
270 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
271 |                                           node.getClass() +
272 |                                           " type: " +
273 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
274 |         }
275 |       case Token.WHILE:
276 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
277 |       case Token.WITH:
278 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
279 |     }
280 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
281 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1539 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1540 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1541 |       return getString();
1542 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1543 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1544 |       if (left == null) {
1545 |         return null;
1546 |       }
1547 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1548 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1549 |       return "this";
1550 |     } else {
1551 |       return null;
1552 |     }
1553 |   }

463 |       if (previous != null) {

465 |       }
466 |     }
467 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
468 |       InputId id = input.getInputId();
469 |       CompilerInput previous = inputsById.put(id, input);
470 |       if (previous != null) {
471 |         report(JSError.make(DUPLICATE_INPUT, input.getName()));
472 |       }

===== M0964 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 79-140
Documentation:

Method code:
79 |   @Override
80 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType other) {
81 |     if (!(other instanceof ArrowType)) {
82 |       return false;
83 |     }
84 | 
85 |     ArrowType that = (ArrowType) other;
86 | 
87 |     // this.returnType <: that.returnType (covariant)
88 |     if (!this.returnType.isSubtype(that.returnType)) {
89 |       return false;
90 |     }
91 |     // that.paramType[i] <: this.paramType[i] (contravariant)
92 |     // TODO(nicksantos): This is incorrect. It should be invariant.
93 |     // Follow up with closure team on how to fix this without everyone
94 |     // hating on us.
95 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
96 |     Node thatParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
97 |     while (thisParam != null && thatParam != null) {
98 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
99 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
100 |         JSType thatParamType = thatParam.getJSType();
101 |         if (thatParamType == null ||
102 |             !thatParamType.isSubtype(thisParamType)) {
103 |           return false;
104 |         }
105 |       }
106 |       boolean thisIsVarArgs = thisParam.isVarArgs();
107 |       boolean thatIsVarArgs = thatParam.isVarArgs();
108 |       // don't advance if we have variable arguments
109 |       if (!thisIsVarArgs) {
110 |         thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
111 |       }
112 |       if (!thatIsVarArgs) {
113 |         thatParam = thatParam.getNext();
114 |       }
115 |       // both var_args indicates the end
116 |       if (thisIsVarArgs && thatIsVarArgs) {
117 |         thisParam = null;
118 |         thatParam = null;
119 |       }
120 |     }
121 | 
122 |     // Right now, the parser's type system doesn't have a good way
123 |     // to model optional arguments.
124 |     //
125 |     // Suppose we have
126 |     // function f(number, number) {}
127 |     // function g(number) {}
128 |     // If the second arg of f is optional, then f is a subtype of g,
129 |     // but g is not a subtype of f.
130 |     // If the second arg of f is required, then g is a subtype of f,
131 |     // but f is not a subtype of g.
132 |     //
133 |     // Until we model optional params, let's just punt on this.
134 |     // If one type has more arguments than the other, we won't check them.
135 |     //
136 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): This is described in Draft 2 of the ES4 spec,
137 |     // Section 3.4.6: Subtyping Function Types. It seems really
138 |     // strange but I haven't thought a lot about the implementation.
139 |     return true;
140 |   }

===== M0968 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 690-787
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
690 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
691 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
692 |     /*
693 |      * This is the case where:
694 |      *
695 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
696 |      * the script.
697 |      *
698 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
699 |      * the caller of the function.
700 |      *
701 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
702 |      * back to the caller of the function.
703 |      *
704 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
705 |      * asked to traverse.
706 |      *
707 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
708 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
709 |      */
710 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
711 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
712 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
713 |       return null;
714 |     }
715 | 
716 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
717 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
718 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
719 |       case Token.IF:
720 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
721 |       case Token.CASE:
722 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
723 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
724 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
725 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
726 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
727 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
728 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
730 |           } else {
731 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
732 |           }
733 |         } else {
734 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
735 |         }
736 |         break;
737 |       case Token.FOR:
738 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
739 |           return parent;
740 |         } else {
741 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
742 |         }
743 |       case Token.WHILE:
744 |       case Token.DO:
745 |         return parent;
746 |       case Token.TRY:
747 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
748 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
749 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
750 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
751 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
752 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
753 |           }
754 |         // CATCH block.
755 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
756 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
757 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
758 |           } else {
759 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
760 |           }
761 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
762 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
763 |           if (cfa != null) {
764 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
765 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
766 |             }
767 |           }
768 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
769 |         }
770 |     }
771 | 
772 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
773 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
774 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
775 | 
776 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
777 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
778 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
779 |     }
780 | 
781 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
782 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
783 |     } else {
784 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
785 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
786 |     }
787 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1545-1580
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1545 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1546 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1547 | 
1548 |     skipEOLs();
1549 | 
1550 |     boolean restArg = false;
1551 |     token = next();
1552 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1553 |       token = next();
1554 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1555 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1556 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, IR.empty());
1557 |       }
1558 |       restArg = true;
1559 |     }
1560 | 
1561 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1562 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1563 |       skipEOLs();
1564 |       if (restArg) {
1565 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1566 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1567 |         next();
1568 |         skipEOLs();
1569 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1570 |       }
1571 | 
1572 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1573 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1574 |       } else {
1575 |         next();
1576 |       }
1577 |     }
1578 | 
1579 |     return typeNode;
1580 |   }

===== M0983 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

   * duplicates.

430 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
431 |       List<JSModule> modules) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1610-1626
Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1610 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1611 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1612 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1613 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1614 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1615 |         next();
1616 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1617 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1618 |           next();
1619 |         }
1620 |         skipEOLs();
1621 |         token = next();
1622 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1623 |       }
1624 |     }
1625 |     return typeExpr;
1626 |   }
